# Diffusion Planner CAMP v16 Iteration Audit

V16 uses public nuScenes data as the source path for fixed TiERIV Diffusion
Planner candidate tensors. DP remains fixed at
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4` and is only a candidate generator.
CAMP may only rerank/select fixed DP candidates. This audit does not authorize
DP training/modification, candidate tensor mutation, trajectory generation,
trajectory repair/rewrite/blend, promotion, deployment, Full36/formal seeds, or
performance/safety claims.

## V16 nuScenes Fixed-DP Candidate Tensor Source Inventory Preflight

The v16 nuScenes fixed-DP candidate tensor source-inventory preflight passed on
AutoDL with exit `0`:

`/root/autodl-tmp/camp_dp_v16_nuscenes_fixed_dp_candidate_tensor_source_inventory_preflight_e8fb15e4cb_20260707T151143CST`

This preflight checked that AutoDL has a readable nuScenes root, registered the
current CAMP nuScenes/trajdata bridge entrypoints, listed fixed-DP inference
input requirements, recorded direct nuScenes fields and adapter gaps, and
pre-registered split, smoke-scale, artifact, timing, and no-go boundaries. It
did not generate candidate tensors, train CAMP, run paired evaluation, mutate
DP/candidates/trajectories, or make a performance claim.

## V16 nuScenes Fixed-DP Candidate Tensor Adapter Plan

The v16 nuScenes fixed-DP candidate tensor adapter plan passed on AutoDL with
exit `0`:

`/root/autodl-tmp/camp_dp_v16_nuscenes_fixed_dp_candidate_tensor_adapter_plan_65945e793f_20260707T153048CST`

This plan maps nuScenes/trajdata sample fields to fixed-DP input surfaces and
pre-registers the smoke gate. It keeps route-like information derived from the
local vector map rather than a mission route, marks traffic-light state as
unknown/unavailable with no safety claim, and requires candidate tensor
shape/hash plus CAMP atom-table hash recording. It did not execute adapter
materialization, candidate generation, training, paired evaluation, DP
modification, candidate mutation, trajectory mutation, or any claim.

and verified before the retry decision:

- `/root/autodl-tmp/camp_dp_v16_nuscenes_fixed_dp_candidate_tensor_smoke_preflight_01b4306d15_20260707T162331CST`

does not contain a v16 smoke execution retry runner/exporter that can prove
`candidate_count=8` fixed-DP candidate tensors for `target_records=256`.

No fake candidate tensor was generated. The retry did not train CAMP, run
paired evaluation, modify DP code/config/weights/checkpoints, mutate candidate
tensors or trajectories, use Full36/formal seeds, report GPU, promote, deploy,
or make a performance/safety claim.

## V16 nuScenes Fixed-DP Candidate Tensor Smoke Execution Retry Runner Remediation

The v16 smoke execution retry runner remediation passed on AutoDL with exit
`0`. CAMP was synchronized with local/GitHub `main` at
`751d1bb2ab3342de36eabf6a1a1e243ba9f4ec5f`, and DP remained fixed at
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`.

`/root/autodl-tmp/camp_dp_v16_nuscenes_fixed_dp_candidate_tensor_smoke_execution_retry_runner_remediation_751d1bb2ab_20260707T190918CST`

The remediation adds the minimal CAMP-side fixed-DP K=8 candidate tensor
runner/exporter:

`scripts/integrations/run_diffusion_planner_dp_camp_v16_nuscenes_fixed_dp_candidate_tensor_exporter.py`

Root cause and remediation:
- The failed retry had no reviewed CAMP-side K=8 exporter and the native
  `valid_predictor.py` path saves one prediction per sample.
- The runner uses fixed DP native sampling via
  `/root/autodl-tmp/Diffusion-Planner/guidance_gui/generate_samples.py`.
- The runner preserves DP Top-1 at `dp_top1_index=0`, samples seven additional
  unguided fixed-DP candidates, and records v14-style provenance/hash fields.
- The blocker-resolution probe carries 32 neighbor rows, while fixed DP expects
  320 neighbor tokens; the CAMP runner zero-pads only the runtime tensor input
  to 320 and keeps the original adapter input shape/hash in provenance.

The artifact executed one real fixed-DP blocker-resolution probe only. It did
not rerun the 256-record smoke retry, train CAMP, run paired evaluation, modify
DP code/config/weights/checkpoints, mutate candidate tensors or trajectories,
use Full36/formal seeds, report GPU, promote, deploy, or make a
performance/safety claim.

It selects `target_records=1024` for the next pilot generation gate and records

The plan input contract is the smoke result-review artifact, the CAMP fixed-DP

ids within the pilot, and generate only a candidate corpus.

synchronized with local/GitHub `main` at

with local/GitHub `main` at `0ebe7a1fe3744bde6084c6ac2596d2458e32d4f8`,
and DP remained fixed at `7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`.

This plan-only gate expands the next candidate-corpus target from the pilot
1024 records / 4 scenes shape to a 10k-record, at-least-30-scene first
scale-up target. It only records target sizing, timing estimates,
source-selection policy, split policy, pass checks, and stop conditions. It

promote, deploy, or make performance/safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

## V16 nuScenes Fixed-DP Candidate Tensor Scale-Up Execution Failure

The v16 scale-up execution gate was started on AutoDL and failed before any
candidate tensor record was exported. CAMP was synchronized at
`f7ae27d0443a7a4600e8280880c30f1764b86fc1`; DP remained fixed at
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`. The failure artifact is:

`/root/autodl-tmp/camp_dp_v16_nuscenes_fixed_dp_candidate_tensor_scaleup_candidates_1503f04e11_20260708T174857CST`

The launch used the extracted trainval metadata root
`/root/autodl-tmp/camp_dp_v16_nuscenes_trainval_metadata_scaleup_f7ae27d0`
with `850` scenes and `34149` samples available, the target `10000` records,
minimum `30` distinct scenes, `K=8`, `candidate_count=8`, and a per-scene cap
of `334`. The output root was absent before launch, and no matching scale-up
execution process was running.

The execution stopped at record `0`. The wrapper created
`inputs/dp_input_000000.npz`, but the existing fixed-DP exporter rejected the
gate before exporting a candidate tensor because its status authorization
allowlist did not include `v16_nuscenes_fixed_dp_candidate_tensor_scaleup_execution_only`.
The record-level exporter report failed `status_authorizes_exporter`.
